## The backlog (operator's 7 UI items + the head-start API work)

1. Tabs shown driven by docked/in-space — **R50**
2. Move ore out of a ship's cargo/ore-hold into the station hangar — **R51**
3. Agent Finder: add a "jumps" limit — **R52**
4. Login-default tab bug (Station selected while in space) — **R50**
5. Hangar inventory shows a fake "of 1,000,000 m³" — show "Room used" only — **R51**
6. Look like the EVE client: condensed, square, no rounded corners — **R53** (last, so the token flip cascades to the new UI)
7. Wallet + Corp Wallet tabs — **R50**
- Then: wire the Tier A/B calls from `docs/api-coverage-plan.md` (head start, no UI required).

## Sequencing decision

Builds serialize (they share `flow.ts`, the store, and `src/server.js` — the conflict that has bitten repeatedly). Order: R50 → R51 → R52 → R53 → drone-cap display fix → Tier A/B API wiring. Read-only research (already done: the API catalog) ran in parallel. **Reasoning:** parallel builds on shared files cause merge chaos; serial is slower but clean, and the operator set this to loop precisely so it runs unattended.

## Standing decisions carried in (already resolved with the operator)

- **Server boundary (hard, reinforced 2026-07-21):** web client only; the sole server surface is the thin bridge (`server/src/_secondary/express/*` + tests). We only ever *permit* calls to handlers that already exist — never implement one. A call with no eve.js handler is flagged, not filled.
- **Stop debugging server rules through the client.** Inert belt rats and rock-depletion were server behaviour, not client bugs. Workers are briefed to note-and-move-on on anything that smells like game mechanics.
- **"Must be in station" bot requirement stays advisory, not blocking** (operator confirmed) — the loops resolve docked/undocked themselves, so a hard block would make the launcher narrower than the bot.

Tab visibility is now a one-place data table (`web/src/ui/tabs.ts`, `where: docked|in-space|both`); the login default derives from the authoritative docked flag (fixes the Station-in-space bug); Wallet renders `account.GetCashBalance`, Corp Wallet renders `account.GetWalletDivisionsInfo`. **R51 — move ore out of a ship bay, drop the fake hangar limit** (web `30ae9e0`; docs this commit). Items 2 + 5. **The real block for item 2 was in the BFF, not the UI:** `resolvePlace` (`src/server.js`) addressed only hangar/cargo/container/corp, so a ship's specialised bays (ore hold, drone bay, …) could not be named as a transfer **source** — that is why the panel offered no move out of them. Added a `{kind:"shipBay", bay:"<key>"}` place resolving the **active ship's** bay by its bay KEY through **R40's `SHIP_BAYS`** (reuse of the same enumeration `/bays` reads with, not a guess); same bind + source location as `cargo`, only the flag differs, and symmetric (valid as a destination too, no extra code). The browser never sends a flag number. UI: `bayIsActionable` generalised from cargo-only to any present bay of the active ship, each bay addressed via `bayPlace(bay)`; `samePlace`/`placeName`/`rowsOf` learned the kind; `refreshOpenPlaces` (`flow.ts`) re-reads the open ship's bays after a mutation; a bay on a hull you are not flying stays read-only. **Item 5:** the station hangar renders **"Room used: {used} m³"** (new `roomUsedText`) — no "of {capacity}", no gauge — keyed **structurally** on being the station-hangar card, never on sniffing the 1,000,000 phantom; ship bays with a real finite capacity keep "X of Y" + gauge. Tests 1608 → 1615 (+7, three watched RED first). **Live (`rrfarmer` → Farmer's Procurer, docked):** moved a Veldspar stack (85,798) `from:{shipBay:"ore"}` → hangar — `applied:true`, server `OnItemChange` flag **134 → 4**, authority re-read confirms it **left the ore hold** and **appears in the hangar** (flag 4); restored it hangar → `{shipBay:"ore"}` so Farmer's fixture is intact; session released, Farmer left docked. `tsc` + `build:web` clean. Verified through the real BFF+EveJS; the browser paint itself was not seen (pane hidden).

**R52 — Agent Finder "within N jumps" limit** (web `ff088c4`; docs this commit). Item 3. **Client-only filter over data the finder already holds** — jumps are computed once by `flow.ts`'s `distancesFrom` BFS from the docked system and carried on every row; this adds one more client-side filter (like the text search), so it never re-hits the server. **The pure logic lives in a new `web/src/ui/agentFinderFilter.ts`, unit-testable, not buried in markup:** `parseJumpsLimit(raw)` (blank/whitespace/negative/non-numeric → `null` = no filter, opt-in — guards the `Number("")===0` trap) and `filterFinderRows(rows, {jumpsLimit, searchText})` (jumps filter first, then the R6a text filter, so the render cap counts real matches). Rule: limit active → reachable AND `jumps <= limit`; unreachable (`jumps null`) excluded when active; same-system (0) passes any limit ≥ 0; blank shows all. `AgentFinder.svelte` gained a numeric "Within jumps" input (placeholder "any", raw-string `oninput` so blank stays blank, does **not** call `find()`) and derives the filtered rows through the module; the summary + empty-state say it plainly ("within N jumps", "unreachable agents are hidden"). Tests **1615 → 1625** (+10, all in the pure module; **5 jumps-specific assertions watched RED first** with the impl stubbed, then green). `tsc` + `build:web` clean. **Live (real BFF static reads + real 5268-system graph + the client's own BFS, `rrfarmer` token, no bridge session):** 2000 courier agents, distances from Jita → counts shrink monotonically as the limit tightens (any 2000 / 30→1931 / 20→1735 / 10→553 / 5→227 / 3→96 / 1→8 / 0→0), each set's max jumps ≤ its limit, unreachable (34) drop out under any limit, tighter sets nested in looser ones. Browser paint not seen (pane hidden); proven by the real data layer + pure filter on live bytes. No server started/restarted; no `EVEJS_*` overrides; all three listeners healthy, characters left untouched (the finder's static reads need no dock).

**R56 — Character Sheet page (API head-start, Tier B #5)** (web this commit; eve.js `579c0573` on `ReconcileEliteMode`; docs this commit). Capstone of the head-start; "who is this character." **Four read-only gateway pairs on existing handlers** (`charMgr.GetPublicInfo3` `:516`, `GetCharacterDescription` `:617`, `GetHomeStation` `:659`, `GetCloneInfo` `:623`) — the three R37 asset `charMgr` pairs were reused, not re-added; `GetPrivateInfo` + `GetRecentShipKillsAndLosses` left refused for later. All top-level, side-effect free, issued **arg-less** by the BFF so `resolveCharacterInfo` falls through to `session.characterID` (no cross-character read). **Real wire shapes, captured live** (`rrfarmer` → Farmer, char 140000005) — the briefs did not guess these, they were read through the BFF first: `GetPublicInfo3` → `{type:"list", items:[util.KeyVal{…}]}` (the row is `items[0]`, a KeyVal — **not** a Rowset); `GetCharacterDescription` → a **bare JS string**; `GetHomeStation` → a **single util.KeyVal** (home stationID + a redundant name/type/system set); `GetCloneInfo` → a single util.KeyVal whose `clones`/`implants` are `{type:"dict", entries:[[key, util.KeyVal], …]}` — **EMPTY** for Farmer's clean clone. Decoder `web/src/bridge/characterSheet.ts` built against exactly those bytes. **R7d — resolve or omit, never leak an id:** corporationID / allianceID / home stationID / implant typeIDs are resolved to names through `/api/names`; **bloodlineID / raceID / ancestryID are OMITTED — not decoded at all** (no `/api/names` kind and no `staticData.js` resolver exists — checked `resolveOneName`; rendering them as numbers is exactly what R7d forbids); securityStatus is a **float**, shown as-is. **The player-corp edge is real and handled:** Farmer's corp 98000001 is a player corp that static `/api/names` answers `null` for (player corps are runtime data, absent from the SDE) — the panel degrades to **"Unknown corporation"**, never the number, and the store caches it as a definitive unknown. **Real sheet read live** (`GET /api/bridge/character-sheet`, all four `errors` null): name **Farmer**, security **+0.14** (0.1404), corporation id 98000001 → **Unknown (no static name — player corp)**, alliance **none**, home station id 60015249 → **"Manifest V - AIR Laboratories Trade Center"** (distinct from the current dock 60000358), bio **"Character created via EveJS Elysian"**, **0 jump clones, 0 implants (clean clone)** — the honest-empty implants path is the only clone path Farmer exercises live; the populated-implants decode is proven by tests. **BFF `/api/bridge/character-sheet`:** four independent `Promise.allSettled` reads, empty ≠ failed, each its own error code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND surfaced. **UI (`CharacterSheet.svelte`, tab `characterSheet` `where:"both"`, in `panelFirstMount`):** name, security (signed/coloured), corp + alliance (alliance row omitted when none), home station, bio (empty bio → honest "not written a bio"), implants (clean clone → honest "no implants"), every value a name via `resolvedName` (never the id). **R57 — Plumbing sweep: top-level reads (fittings / kill rights / LP), PLUMBING ONLY — no UI** (web this commit; eve.js `6e35673c` on `ReconcileEliteMode`; docs this commit). Operator: *"go and do all plumbing, so we can set up the UI easier later."* First batch of the plumbing sweep — the reads are made **reachable + decodable** so a later goal builds UI cheaply; **no panel, no tab, no store slice** ships. **Five read-only gateway pairs on existing handlers, no `Handle_*` change** (`evejsWebGatewayRuntime.js`): `charFittingMgr.GetFittings` (`charFittingMgrService.js:31`, the CHAR service — `super("charFittingMgr")`; corp/allianceFittingMgr are distinct and NOT listed), `bountyProxy.GetMyKillRights` (`bountyProxyService.js:500`), `LPSvc.GetLPsForCharacter` (`lpService.js:86`), `LPSvc.GetLPExchangeRates` (`:81`), `LPSvc.GetAvailableOffersFromCorp` (`:154`). Every pair verified: existing handler, top-level (no `MachoBindObject` on charFittingMgr/bountyProxy; LPSvc has a bind path but its already-allowlisted `GetAllMyCharacterWalletLPBalances` proved the top-level seam in R6, so these ride it), session-scoped (each handler reads `session.characterID` — the BFF issues them **arg-less**, so no cross-character read). Every mutating sibling (SaveFitting/DeleteFitting/SellKillRight/TakeOffer/the LP transfers) stays refused. **NONE skipped** — all five had a handler and were top-level. **REAL WIRE SHAPES, CAPTURED LIVE not guessed** (`rrfarmer` → Farmer, char 140000005, through the three new BFF routes): **fittings** = `{type:"dict", entries:[[<fittingID>, util.KeyVal{description, fitData(`{type:"list"}` of `{type:"tuple", items:[typeID, flagID, quantity]}`), fittingID, name, ownerID, savedDate(`{type:"long"}` FILETIME), shipTypeID}], …]}` — **POPULATED**: Farmer had one saved fitting ("asdf", shipTypeID 588, three modules `[3651,28,1]`/`[21857,19,1]`/`[3636,27,1]`), so this is a real populated capture, not a builder guess. **kill-rights** = `{type:"list", items:[util.KeyVal{killRightID, fromID, toID, expiryTime(long), price(int|null), restrictedTo(int|null)}]}` — Farmer holds none, so the LIVE capture was the **empty list** `{type:"list", items:[]}` (a real "no kill rights" answer); the populated row shape is the server's `buildKillRightPayload` output, mirrored in the test fixture. **LP** = three reads: `GetLPsForCharacter` → `{type:"list", items:[{type:"list", items:[issuerCorpID, loyaltyPoints]}, …]}` **POPULATED** (`[1000002,213]`, `[1000033,1500]`, `[1000035,100067000]`); `GetLPExchangeRates` and `GetAvailableOffersFromCorp` → `{type:"list", items:[]}`, **EMPTY-BY-DESIGN** (both handlers return `buildList([])` with no seed data — a legitimate "no rates / no offers yet" state, not a bug; the plumbing lands for when data exists). **DECODERS from those bytes** (`web/src/bridge/{fittings,killRights,lpStore}.ts`), tuple/list/long/KeyVal read through the R32 helpers, FILETIME + LP amounts bigint-safe (never through `Number`). **R7d at the decoder level (not rendering — this is plumbing):** every id survives AS A NUMERIC FIELD for a future UI to resolve — fitting shipTypeID / module typeIDs / slot flagID / ownerID, kill-right fromID/toID/restrictedTo, LP issuerCorpID — none forced into a label, none lost. LP/offers/rates decoders pass raw items through without imposing a schema (their populated shape is unknown). **BFF routes:** `/api/bridge/fittings` + `/api/bridge/kill-rights` (single held-session reads) and `/api/bridge/lp` (three independent `Promise.allSettled` reads, empty ≠ failed, each its own error code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND surfaced). **NO UI, NO store slice, NO panel/tab** — `public/dist` unchanged (the decoders are tree-shaken, nothing imports them yet). **TESTS, WATCHED FAILING FIRST:** decoders (`fittings.test.ts` +5, `killRights.test.ts` +5, `lpStore.test.ts` +7 — the real captured bytes, empty paths, bigint-safety, tuple/array tolerance, R7d id-survival sweeps with matcher-proof companions) + BFF route test (`test/bridgeR57Reads.test.js` +6 — each route dispatches the right service/method top-level arg-less on the held session, empty ≠ failed, one failed LP read carries its own code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND 404, NO_LIVE_SESSION 409); the RED-first proof perturbed one load-bearing line per decoder (wrong tuple index / wrong key / wrong pair index) → **7 real-bytes assertions went RED**, then each reverted. The other agent's `ReconcileEliteMode` destiny work (npcBehaviorLoop / phase-9 NPC-chase-speed command) was left fully intact — only my two eve.js files committed, by pathspec; `git status` verified after (their 3 modified + 3 untracked files remain). Farmer left docked at 60000358, session released; no `EVEJS_*` overrides beyond the canonical intercept flag; all three listeners healthy. **NOTES, STATED PLAINLY:** no UI was built or seen (this goal has none — verification is the BFF routes + decoder tests against real bytes); kill-rights and LP rates/offers were only exercised on their **empty** path live (a real state for this world) — the populated kill-right decode is proven by the builder-shaped fixture + tests; I deliberately did **not** restart EveJS a second time after committing — the running 17812 already loaded the committed pairs (I restarted after editing, before commit), and a second restart would have pulled the other agent's in-flight *uncommitted* destiny changes into the shared server.

**R58 — Plumbing sweep: charMgr social/profile reads, PLUMBING ONLY — no UI** (web this commit; eve.js `511a9745` on `ReconcileEliteMode`; docs this commit). Second batch of the plumbing sweep, same contract as R57 (allowlist pair + BFF passthrough + decoder from real bytes + tests; NO panel/tab/store slice; update the snapshot; restart EveJS). **Eleven read-only gateway pairs on existing handlers, no `Handle_*` change** (`evejsWebGatewayRuntime.js`) — every one verified TOP-LEVEL on `charMgr` (a `Handle_*` on `CharMgrService`, not on the R37 bound global-assets object; the assets bind stays the ONLY bound charMgr path) and session-scoped (`resolveCharacterInfo`/`sessionCharacterID` fall through to `session.characterID`): `GetPublicInfo` (`charMgrService.js:510`), `GetHomeStationRow` (`:666`), `GetCharacterCreationDate` (`:707`), `GetSettingsInfo` (`:723`), `GetContactList` (`:730`), `GetOwnerNoteLabels` (`:987`), `GetOwnerNote` (`:1001`), `GetNote` (`:1040`), `GetPaperdollState` (`:700`), `GetCohortsForCharacter` (`:553`), `GetPrivateInfoOnCorpChange` (`:602`). **ONE SKIPPED BY POLICY:** `GetRecentShipKillsAndLosses` (`:559`) — the handler exists but the allowlist comment reserves it "for a later goal"; I honoured the reservation, left it unwired, and the refusal sweep now names it (plus `GetPrivateInfo`, also still refused). Every mutating charMgr sibling (AddContact/DeleteContacts/BlockOwners, the note writers Add/Edit/Remove OwnerNote + SetNote, the label editors, SetActivityStatus, SetCharacterDescription) stays refused. **REAL WIRE SHAPES, CAPTURED LIVE not guessed** (`rrfarmer` → Farmer, char 140000005, through the three new BFF routes; every shape below is the byte capture, not a builder guess): **GetPublicInfo** = a **bare** `util.KeyVal` (the older public-info shape — distinct from R56's `GetPublicInfo3`, which wraps the same KeyVal in a list) carrying characterID/name/corp(98000001)/alliance(null)/faction(500001)/race/bloodline/ancestry/gender/create+start FILETIMEs(long)/description/securityStatus(0.1404 float)/bounty/station(60000358)/system. **GetHomeStationRow** = a `util.KeyVal` (delegates to `GetHomeStation`; home station 60015249, type 92885, system 30100032 — distinct from the current dock). **GetCharacterCreationDate** = `{type:"long", value:"134274243893290000"}`. **GetSettingsInfo** = a bare 2-tuple `[{type:"Buffer", data:[…80 bytes…]}, 0]` — an opaque py2 codeobject; the decoder CARRIES the bytes, never interprets them. **GetContactList** = `util.KeyVal{addresses: Rowset[contactID, inWatchlist, relationshipID, labelMask], blocked: Rowset[senderID]}` — **EMPTY** for Farmer (both rowsets `lines:[]`; a legitimate "no contacts / none blocked" state); rowset lines are **bare arrays** (not KeyVals/packedrows). **GetOwnerNoteLabels** = `Rowset[noteID, label]` — **NOT empty**: the handler lazily seeds a default `[1, "S:Folders"]` note, so this always carries at least that row (a real handler side effect of the read, noted). **GetOwnerNote(1)** = `{type:"list", items:[util.KeyVal{noteID:1, label:"S:Folders", note:"1::F::0::Main|"}]}` (the folders note). **GetNote(1)** = a **bare string** `""` (Farmer keeps no entity note — a real "no note" answer). **GetPaperdollState** = bare int `0`. **GetCohortsForCharacter** = `{type:"list", items:[]}` — **EMPTY-BY-DESIGN** (the handler always returns `buildList([])`). **GetPrivateInfoOnCorpChange** = a `CachedMethodCallResult` whose `name` is a `{type:"rawstr"}` and whose inner `util.KeyVal{corporationID:98000001, corporationDateTime(long)}` is **NESTED in `args[1].value` (a `substream`)**, NOT at the top level — the crux the decoder unwraps (a top-level KeyVal read would get nothing). **DECODERS from those bytes** (`web/src/bridge/{contactList,characterNotes,characterProfile}.ts`); a new shared `readRowsetRows` in `wire.ts` reads the bare-array Rowset shape (columns→cells by index, tolerant of a `util.Row` list-wrapper too). Longs (FILETIMEs) bigint, ISK/labelMask bigint-safe strings (never through `Number`). **R7d at the decoder level (plumbing, not rendering):** every id survives AS A NUMERIC FIELD for a future UI to resolve — publicInfo corp/alliance/faction/station/system/type/race/bloodline/ancestry, contact contactID/senderID, corpChange corporationID, home stationID/typeID — none forced into a label, none lost; cohorts pass raw items through without a schema (populated shape unknown). **BFF routes:** `/api/bridge/character-profile` (seven independent `Promise.allSettled` reads), `/api/bridge/character-notes` (three reads; `?noteID=`/`?itemID=` default 1 so a UI can page notes), `/api/bridge/contact-list` (single read) — empty ≠ failed, each read its own error code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND surfaced. **NO UI, NO store slice, NO panel/tab** — `public/dist` unchanged (decoders tree-shaken, nothing imports them). **TESTS, WATCHED FAILING FIRST:** decoders (`contactList.test.ts` +7, `characterNotes.test.ts` +8, `characterProfile.test.ts` +13 — real captured bytes, the empty-but-legitimate paths, the substream unwrap, bigint-safety, bare-array rowset decode, R7d id-survival sweeps with matcher-proof companions) + BFF route test (`test/bridgeR58Reads.test.js` +7 — each route dispatches the right charMgr methods top-level, arg-less or with the query note ids, empty ≠ failed, one failed profile read carries its own code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND 404, NO_LIVE_SESSION 409); the RED-first proof perturbed one load-bearing line each in `readRowsetRows` (column index +1), `characterNotes` (note→label), `characterProfile` (corpChange substream key), `contactList` (contactID→labelMask) → **8 real-bytes assertions went RED**, then each reverted. A transcription slip in the settings-bytes fixture (81 vs the real 80) was caught by the failing test and corrected to the real capture. Combined `node --test` **1717 → 1752** (+35). `tsc` + `build:web` clean. **eve.js snapshot** (`webGatewayServiceCall`, isolated runner) brought current with the eleven R58 pairs; the refusal sweep lost `charMgr.GetPublicInfo` (now allowlisted) and gained `GetPrivateInfo` + `GetRecentShipKillsAndLosses` (still refused) — green **8/8**. **EveJS restarted** (17812 → 60744) detached via PowerShell `Start-Process` (canonical `EVEJS_PROXY_LOCAL_INTERCEPT=1`, from `eve.js/server`, so it survives the shell — the process-ownership trap) to load the pairs; **web BFF restarted** (42388 → 73304) for the routes; market daemon (54808) untouched; all pairs verified LIVE through the three routes before commit. The other agent's `ReconcileEliteMode` destiny work (now a phase-9 unreachable-motion reclassification: a new untracked test + parity evidence json) was left fully intact — only my two eve.js files committed, by pathspec; `git status` verified after (their 2 untracked files remain). Farmer left docked at 60000358 (home 60015249), session released via `/api/logout`; no `EVEJS_*` overrides beyond the canonical intercept flag; all three listeners healthy. **NOTES, STATED PLAINLY:** no UI was built or seen (this goal has none — verification is the BFF routes + decoder tests against real bytes); contacts/blocked/cohorts and the entity note were exercised only on their **empty-but-legitimate** path live (a real state for this world) — the populated contact/note decodes are proven by builder-shaped fixtures + tests; `GetOwnerNoteLabels`/`GetContactList` lazily seed a default folders note on read (the handler's own behaviour, permitted unchanged — not a write I added); I did **not** restart EveJS a second time after committing (the running 60744 already loaded the committed pairs — restarted after editing, before commit — and a second restart would have risked the other agent's in-flight work).

**R59 — Plumbing sweep: comms reads (mail aux / notifications / calendar), PLUMBING ONLY — no UI** (web this commit; eve.js `20004361` on `ReconcileEliteMode`; docs this commit). Third batch of the plumbing sweep, same contract as R57/R58 (allowlist pair + BFF passthrough + decoder from real bytes + tests; NO panel/tab/store slice; update the snapshot; restart EveJS). **Eleven read-only gateway pairs on existing handlers, no `Handle_*` change** (`evejsWebGatewayRuntime.js`) across FOUR services, each grep-confirmed to exist and verified TOP-LEVEL (machoNet binds every one as `["<svc>", null]` — unbound; the second tuple element is the bind context and `null` = plain `/call`): **mail aux (5)** `mailMgr.GetLabels` (`mailMgrService.js:398`), `mailingListsMgr.GetJoinedLists`/`GetInfo`/`GetMembers`/`GetSettings` (`mailingListsMgrService.js:118/124/170/238` — a DISTINCT service from mailMgr); **notifications (3)** `notificationMgr.GetAllNotifications`/`GetByGroupID`/`GetUnprocessed` (`:61/75/89`); **calendar (4)** `calendarMgr.GetResponsesForCharacter`/`GetResponsesToEvent` (`:150/156`), `calendarProxy.GetEventList`/`GetEventDetails` (`:13/20`). **⚠ THE FLAGGED calendarProxy BINDING — NOT DEFERRED, WIRED.** The sweep brief warned calendarProxy might be a retail `ProxySvc` needing a different seam; grepped it and it is a **plain `BaseService`** (`class CalendarProxyService extends BaseService { super("calendarProxy") }`, plain `Handle_*` methods) registered `["calendarProxy", null]` in `machoNetService.js` — it answers plain top-level `/call` exactly like calendarMgr, **confirmed live** (`GetEventList` returned a real event through `/api/bridge/calendar`). So both calendarProxy pairs landed; **NONE of the eleven were skipped.** Every mutating sibling (the mail label writers + every mailingLists writer, the notification Mark*/Delete* writers, the calendar Create*/Edit*/Delete*/SendEventResponse writers) stays refused. **REAL WIRE SHAPES, CAPTURED LIVE not guessed** (`rrfarmer` → Farmer, char 140000005, through the three new BFF routes): **notifications** = a **BARE JSON ARRAY** (not a `{type:"list"}` wrapper — the handler returns the mapped array directly) of `util.KeyVal{notificationID, typeID, senderID, receiverID, processed(bool), created(long FILETIME), data}` — **POPULATED**: Farmer has **213** notifications across 22 typeIDs (211 unprocessed), `created` a FILETIME past 2^53 (`"134282765436910000"`) so **bigint**, and `data` a **per-typeID marshaled dict** (keys differ by type: itemID/payout, or billID/amount/debtorID/…) **carried through untouched** — the decoder imposes no schema on it. `GetByGroupID(0)` returns all 213 (groupID 0 → no filter, `toPositiveInteger(0)||null` = null). **calendar** = `GetResponsesForCharacter` → `{type:"list", items:[]}` **EMPTY** (Farmer responded to nothing); `GetEventList(month,year)` → a **3-tuple** `[{type:"list", items:[<event KeyVal>]}, null, null]` — **POPULATED** for 2026-07: one real event `util.KeyVal{eventID:980000000006, ownerID:1, eventDateTime(long), eventDuration:11, eventTitle:"Make a wish", importance:0, dateModified(long), isDeleted:false, flag:8, autoEventType:null}`; `GetResponsesToEvent(eventID,ownerID)` → `{type:"list", items:[util.KeyVal{characterID, status}]}` — **EMPTY** live even for the real event (no one responded); `GetEventDetails(eventID,ownerID)` → `util.KeyVal{eventText:"The clock reads 11:11 — make a wish.", creatorID:1}` — **POPULATED** (captured by re-hitting the route with the real eventID 980000000006 discovered in the event list). ⚠ With the default `eventID=0` (Farmer's no-event case) `GetResponsesToEvent`/`GetEventDetails` **refuse** ("no such event") — surfaced as that read's error code (`CALL_REFUSED`), NOT a route failure, a legitimate outcome. **mail aux** = **ENTIRELY EMPTY** for Farmer (belongs to no lists, no custom labels): `GetLabels`/`GetJoinedLists`/`GetMembers` → `{type:"dict", entries:[]}`, `GetInfo`/`GetSettings` → `null` — the real "no labels / no lists" state; the populated shapes (dict of `util.KeyVal{labelID,name,color}`; dict of `util.KeyVal{id,name,displayName,isMuted,isOperator,isOwner}`; dict of memberID→accessLevel; settings KeyVal with a nested access dict) mirror the server builders (`buildLabelKeyVal`/`buildMailingListInfoKeyVal`/`buildMailingListSettingsKeyVal`) using the same dict/KeyVal wire primitives proven live in the notification+calendar captures. **DECODERS from those bytes** (`web/src/bridge/{notifications,calendar,mailAux}.ts`); a new shared `readDictPairs` in `wire.ts` iterates an id-keyed marshaled dict (labels/members/access). Longs (FILETIMEs) bigint; `data`/raw items carried through untouched. **R7d at the decoder level (plumbing, not rendering):** every id survives AS A NUMERIC FIELD — notification senderID/receiverID + ids inside `data`; calendar ownerID/creatorID/characterID/eventID; mail labelID/listID/memberID/access entityID — none forced into a label, none dropped. **BFF routes:** `/api/bridge/mail-aux` (five `Promise.allSettled` reads; `?listID=` default 0), `/api/bridge/notifications` (three reads; `?fromID=`/`?groupID=` default 0), `/api/bridge/calendar` (four reads; `?month=`/`?year=` default the current UTC month, `?eventID=`/`?ownerID=` default 0/null) — empty ≠ failed, each read its own error code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND surfaced. **NO UI, NO store slice, NO panel/tab** — `public/dist` unchanged (decoders tree-shaken, nothing imports them). **LIVE-BYTES PROOF BEYOND FIXTURES:** ran the actual decoder functions over the raw live-capture JSON — `decodeNotifications` → 213/211 decoded, `created` bigint preserved; `decodeEventList` → the "Make a wish" event with bigint FILETIMEs; `decodeEventDetails` → text+creator; the empty mail/response paths → `[]`/`null`. **TESTS, WATCHED FAILING FIRST:** decoders (`notifications.test.ts` +8, `calendar.test.ts` +12, `mailAux.test.ts` +11 — verbatim live DTOs, the empty-but-legitimate paths, bigint `created`/FILETIMEs, `data` carried through, R7d id-survival sweeps with matcher-proof companions) + BFF route test (`test/bridgeR59Reads.test.js` +9 — each route dispatches the right service/method top-level with the right args, defaults, empty ≠ failed, one failed read carries its own code, SESSION_NOT_FOUND 404, NO_LIVE_SESSION 409); the RED-first proof perturbed one load-bearing line each in `notifications` (senderID key), `calendar` (eventTitle key), `mailAux` (labelID key), and the route args in `server.js` (GetEventList month/year order, GetByGroupID arg) → real-bytes assertions went RED, then each reverted.
