## SECURITY — /api/bridge/call arg-injection audit (2026-07-22)

**Found while verifying R71.** The web BFF's primary call path `POST /api/bridge/call` (`src/server.js:281`) forwards the browser's `args` VERBATIM to the gateway for any allowlisted pair (pinning only `userid`). So every allowlisted READ must be safe under attacker-chosen args — but several handlers read a **caller-supplied entity id with no ownership check**, letting a logged-in browser read another entity's private data by injecting its id. The canonical case: `ship.GetShipConfiguration` (`shipService.js:1771`: `shipID = args[0] ?? sessionShip`, no check). The R71 worker's "arg-less dedicated route" mitigation was illusory — `/api/bridge/call` bypasses the dedicated route.

**Ran an adversarial audit workflow (`wf_e494de1e-2b6`, 14 classifier agents + synthesis) over ALL 292 allowlisted pairs.** Result: **14 confirmed arg-injection read-leaks** (217 SAFE, 58 out-of-scope writes/binds, 3 need a live probe). High-sensitivity data among them: private corp asset/office locations (`corpmgr.GetAssetInventory`/`…ForLocation`), full/courier contracts incl. item lists (`contractProxy.GetContract`/`GetContractListForOwner`), clones+implants+home station (`charMgr.GetCloneInfo`/`GetHomeStation`/`GetHomeStationRow`), private industry jobs (`industryManager.GetJob`/`GetJobsByOwner`/`GetJobCounts`), mailing-list rosters+ACLs (`mailingListsMgr.GetMembers`/`GetSettings`), plus `ship.GetShipConfiguration` and low-sensitivity `charMgr.GetPaperdollState`. No wallet-balance or mail-body leak (those are session-scoped). The SPA itself never calls the 14 via generic `/api/bridge/call` — it uses session-scoping dedicated routes.

**OPERATOR DECISION (surfaced + answered 2026-07-22): flag-only, keep PRE-PLUMBED, fix server-side via a handoff doc, keep going.** So: **no de-allowlisting, no bridge guard, nothing broken** — the 14 pairs stay allowlisted + routed for future UI. The fix is server-side handler ownership checks, fully specified for another session in **`docs/arg-injection-leak-handoff.md`** (per-pair handler location, the trusted arg, Group A [generic-call only] vs Group B [route also passes a browser query param], a copy-this ownership pattern, and a two-account verification recipe). Lesson captured in memory `[[api-bridge-call-arg-injection]]`. The sweep resumes (R72+).

## R72 — plumbing sweep: the five GATEWAY-BIND reads (Phase-2 prerequisites) (2026-07-22)

**R72 — all 5 gateway binds LANDED, none skipped (eve.js this commit on `ReconcileEliteMode`; web this commit). PLUMBING ONLY — no UI, no writes, NO bound methods. This CLOSES Phase-1 top-level reads.** Fifteenth batch. Unlike every prior batch these return a **bound-object HANDLE (Moniker / OID substruct), not decodable rows** — the deliverable is the allowlist pair + a BFF passthrough that returns the handle envelope INTACT + a thin reachability decoder. A later Phase-2 batch consumes each handle. Combined web tests **2061 → 2066** (+5); `tsc` + `build:web` clean; eve.js snapshot green **8/8** (isolated runner).

- **THE 5 PAIRS LANDED — none skipped** (all `Handle_*` grep-confirmed top-level). Mirrors the already-wired `invbroker.MachoBindObject` two-step (the OID is confined to the gateway; the BFF holds an opaque `boundHandle`; only the bind pair is wired, never a bound method). **skillMgr2.GetMySkillHandler** (`skills/skillMgr2Service.js:8`), **dogmaIM.MachoBindObject** (`dogma/dogmaService.js:9601`), **entity.MachoBindObject** (`drone/entityService.js:25`), **scanMgr.GetSystemScanMgr** (`exploration/scanMgrService.js:1534`), **fleetObjectHandler.MachoBindObject** (`fleets/fleetObjectHandlerService.js:106`).
- **⚠ SAFETY CLASSIFICATION per bind (read the handler for each; 4 safe, 1 flagged):**
  - **skillMgr2.GetMySkillHandler — SESSION-DERIVED, SAFE.** Returns `Moniker("skillHandler", null, session.characterID, null)` — bindParam is the session's OWN charID, `args` ignored. A `GetXxx` returning a Moniker (not an "N=" OID), so it rides top-level `/call`, not the bind two-step. Phase-2 skill reads address service `"skillHandler"`.
  - **dogmaIM.MachoBindObject — SESSION-SCOPED, SAFE.** `buildBoundObjectResponse` registers the caller bindParameter against the OID, BUT every dogma bound read resolves the active ship from the SESSION (`_getShipID(session)`, :1089) and never consults `getBoundObjectParams`/`currentBoundObjectID` — the registered param is inert. (`GetAllInfo`, the undock bootstrap top-level read, stays REFUSED — only the bind is opened.)
  - **entity.MachoBindObject — SESSION-SCOPED, SAFE.** The handler IGNORES bindParams, mints a fresh boundId, and the bound `Cmd*` orders act on the session's own scene. (The bound drone WRITES are Phase-4, not wired.)
  - **scanMgr.GetSystemScanMgr — SESSION-DERIVED, SAFE.** `_ensureSystemParity(session)` + `buildBoundObjectSubstruct("scanMgr", session)` — the target is the session's CURRENT system, server-derived. A `GetXxx` but returns a real OID substruct, so it DOES ride the bind two-step.
  - **⚠ fleetObjectHandler.MachoBindObject — BINDS-ARBITRARY-OID, FLAGGED + WIRED.** `Handle_MachoBindObject` takes `bindParams[0]` as the `fleetID` (fallback `session.fleetid`) with **no membership check** and stores it in the bound context; the Phase-2 bound reads honor it via `_resolveFleetIDFromSession`, and `fleetRuntime.getWings`/`getMotd`/`getFleetComposition` (fleetRuntime.js:1163-1200) take a **bare fleetID** and return that fleet's roster with no gate (`ensureFleetExists` even fabricates one). The bind is the retail prereq so it is wired; the BFF binds it session-scoped (`fleetBindSpec()` passes `args:[]`), but `/api/bridge/call` forwards args verbatim so a browser could bind a foreign fleetID directly. **The leak lives on the Phase-2 bound READ** — appended a full addendum to `docs/arg-injection-leak-handoff.md` (fix = validate `bindParams[0]` against the session's fleet, OR gate each bound read on membership; do NOT allowlist any `fleetObjectHandler` bound read until then).

## R73 — plumbing sweep: the FIRST Phase-2 BOUND-READ batch — 13 skill reads (RB-SKILL) (2026-07-22)

**R73 — all 13 skill bound reads LANDED, none skipped (eve.js this commit on `ReconcileEliteMode`; web this commit). PLUMBING ONLY — no UI, no writes. This is the FIRST Phase-2 bound-read batch and establishes the bound-read pattern for RB-DOGMA/RB-SCAN/RB-INV/etc.** Sixteenth batch. Hangs off the R72 skill-handler gateway. Combined web tests **2066 → 2083** (+17); `tsc` + `build:web` clean; eve.js snapshot green **8/8** (isolated runner).

- **RESOLVED WIRE PATH — top-level `/call` on service `skillHandler`, NOT a bound two-step (grep-proven, then live-proven).** `skillMgr2.GetMySkillHandler` (`skillMgr2Service.js:8`) returns `Moniker("skillHandler", null, session.characterID, null)`. A Moniker (not an `"N="` OID substruct) rides the ORDINARY `/call` seam — no `/bound/bind` step. `"skillHandler"` is a REAL registered service: `skillHandlerService.js` is `class SkillHandlerService extends SkillMgrService { this._name = "skillHandler"; }`, and `eve.js/server/index.js`'s `loadServices` auto-registers every `*Service.js` under its `.name`, so `serviceManager.register(new SkillHandlerService())` keys it as `skillHandler`. The gateway dispatch is `serviceManager.lookup(call.service).callMethod(...)` (`evejsWebGatewayRuntime.js:4350/4384`), so a call to `{skillHandler, GetSkills}` resolves to the SkillHandlerService instance and runs the `Handle_GetSkills` INHERITED from `skillMgrService.js`. **Wired on `skillHandler` (NOT `skillMgr`)** — this keeps `skillMgr`'s browser surface exactly its one WRITE (`SaveNewQueue`) with every `skillMgr` read still refused, so `webGatewaySkills.test.js` (which asserts `skillMgr.GetSkillQueue`/`GetMySkillInfo`/`GetSkillQueueAndFreePoints` refused + "exactly one skillMgr pair") stays green untouched.
- **THE 13 PAIRS LANDED — none skipped** (each `Handle_*` grep-confirmed in `skillMgrService.js`): `GetSkills`(:317), `GetAllSkills`(:322), `GetAttributes`(:312), `GetSkillHistory`(:280), `GetSkillChangesForISIS`(:297), `GetRespecInfo`(:421), `GetFreeSkillPoints`(:469), `GetBoosters`(:448), `GetImplants`(:453), `CheckInjectionConstraints`(:541), `GetSkillPoints`(:302), `GetDiminishedSpFromInjectors`(:552), `GetSkillQueue`(:275). Every SP-SPENDING sibling (SaveNewQueue/AbortTraining/InjectSkillpoints/InjectSkillIntoBrain/ExtractSkills/PurchaseSkills/ApplyFreeSkillPoints*/Split/CombineSkillInjector) is DELIBERATELY ABSENT under `skillHandler` too — reads only.
- **⚠ OWNERSHIP / ARG-INJECTION — OWN-SKILLS-ONLY, NO LEAK, NO HANDOFF FLAG (verified LIVE cross-account).** Static: every one of the 13 derives the character from the SESSION via `SkillMgrService._getCharacterId` → `getCharacterIDFromSession(session)` (`skillQueueRuntime.js:208`: reads only `session.characterID||charid||userid`, never args) and NONE reads a caller charID from args. The two arg-taking reads take an injector item/type id + counts, NOT a charID: `CheckInjectionConstraints(itemID, qty)` resolves the injector against the SESSION's own items (`resolveOwnedSkillTradingItem(session, characterID, itemID)` — throws `SkillTradingItemNotFound` if not owned) and `GetDiminishedSpFromInjectors(typeID, qty, nonDim)` returns 0 for a non-injector typeID. Live: logged in `test2` → **GM Elysian (140000004)**, selected, and called EACH read via `/api/bridge/call` with **Farmer's charID 140000005 injected as the leading arg**. Result — for ALL 13, `injected(Farmer charID) === own baseline` (GM Elysian's own data); `GetSkills` injected returned entries with ownerID EXCLUSIVELY `140000004` (never Farmer's `140000005`); injected `GetSkillPoints` returned **384036** (GM Elysian's own), NOT Farmer's **641792000**. The reads where `injected == Farmer` too (attributes/history/respec/freeSP/boosters/implants/queue/checkInjection) are only where both characters share identical benign DEFAULTS (empty/zeroed), confirmed by `injected == own` for those. **Verdict: own-skills-only for all 13; nothing appended to `docs/arg-injection-leak-handoff.md`.**
- **REAL WIRE SHAPES, CAPTURED LIVE (`rrfarmer` → Farmer 140000005, a MAXED char — 511 skills at V):**
  - `GetSkills` / `GetAllSkills` are IDENTICAL `{type:"dict", entries:[[typeID, objectex1 CharacterSkillEntry]]}` — 511 rows. Each `objectex1`: `header = [ {token …CharacterSkillEntry}, [typeID, trainedSkillLevel, trainedSkillPoints, skillRank, virtualSkillLevel], {type:"dict", entries:[itemID, ownerID, locationID, flagID, groupID, categoryID, groupName, published, inTraining]} ]`, plus empty `list`/`dict`. (Mapped against `skillTransport.js buildCharacterSkillEntry`.)
  - `GetAttributes` `{type:"dict", entries:[[164,20],[165,20],[166,20],[167,20],[168,20]]}` (attributeID → points; 164=cha 165=int 166=mem 167=per 168=wil).
  - `GetSkillPoints` a **BARE number `641792000`** — the total SP. ⚠ BIGINT: a maxed char exceeds 2^53, kept as an EXACT decimal string, never Number.

BFF `boundCall(planetBindSpec(planetID), …)`.

LSC (1, `services/chat/lscService.js`): `SendMessage`(:80). All TOP-LEVEL, carried by `heldTopLevelCall`. Reused the R58/R70 charMgr/charUnboundMgr BFF route file (`src/server.js`) + R87's `requireWriteConfirmation`/`bridgeIDList`/`dispatchBridgeWrite` helpers.

**Confirm-gate pattern (reused R87 `requireWriteConfirmation`):** each of the 18 `POST /api/bridge/{character,chat}/*` routes refuses `400 CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED` (NO dispatch) unless the body carries `confirm: true`. The three extra-care writes (`CreateCharacterWithDoll` → `/character/create-with-doll`; `LSC.SendMessage` → `/chat/send-message`; `CancelCharacterDeletePrepare` → `/character/cancel-delete-prepare`) carry the same gate + an explicit-confirmation message and were **NEVER fired live** — reachability + refusal only.

**Decisions made autonomously (fast mode):**
- **Educated-guess responses, no live byte-capture.** Every charMgr writer returns `null` server-side except `AddOwnerNote` (a new noteID); charUnboundMgr `ToggleValidation` returns a bool and `CreateCharacterWithDoll` the new characterID; the rest return `null`; `LSC.SendMessage` returns `null`. Acks are `{ok, applied}` (+`result` for the number-returning ones); a panel re-reads to prove the mutation. Decoders `decodeCharWriteAck`+`decodeOwnerNoteCreatedAck` (`characterProfile.ts`), `decodeCharUnboundWriteAck`+`decodeCharacterCreatedAck` (`charAccount.ts`), `decodeChatSendAck` (`chat.ts`) — LOCAL coercions only; stayed off `market*.ts`. +10 basic decoder tests (fast mode; skipped the reads-era fail-first + live-trigger grind).
- **⚠ ARG-INJECTION — FIRST WRITE-SIDE FLAG (handoff doc #31-#32).** `UpdateCharacterGender(charId, genderID)` and `UpdateCharacterBloodline(charId, bloodlineID)` take a caller-supplied `charId` (`args[0]`) and mutate THAT record via `updateCharacterRecord` (`characterState.js:1048`) — which looks up by BARE charId and `writeCharacterRecord`s with **no `characterBelongsToAccount`/session check** (source-confirmed). A browser can flip a FOREIGN character's gender / race / bloodline / portrait. Creation-flow writes; the tell is the MISSING userid arg vs the correctly-scoped `CancelCharacterDeletePrepare(charId, session.userid)`. **Fourth batch under the OPERATOR-OWNS-EVEJS protocol — NO server restart, NO live smoke.** Forty-sixth batch, eighteenth WRITE batch, ninth Phase-4, off TWO established binds (no new MachoBindObject pair). Client + bridge; 11 write pairs on existing handlers; no `Handle_*` change; NO panel/tab/store slice; market files untouched. **All 11 LANDED, none skipped** — every `Handle_*` grep-confirmed present: planetMgr (`Handle_UserUpdateNetwork`:1091, `Handle_UserLaunchCommodities`:1172, `Handle_UserTransferCommodities`:1226, `Handle_UserAbandonPlanet`:1259 in `planet/planetMgrService.js`); beyonce (`Handle_CmdGotoPoint`:2420, `Handle_CmdGotoBookmark`:2433, `Handle_CmdAbandonLoot`:2558, `Handle_CmdFleetTagTarget`:2572, `Handle_CmdJumpThroughFleet`:2198, `Handle_BookmarkLocation`:3299, `Handle_BookmarkScanResult`:3325 in `ship/beyonceService.js`).

**BIND MECHANISM — TWO NEW dispatch helpers, TWO existing binds.** WB-PI → NEW `dispatchBoundPlanetWrite(req,res,next,planetID,method,args,kwargs)` → `boundCall(held, webSessionID, planetBindSpec(planetID), method, args, kwargs)` off the R77 `planetMgr.MachoBindObject(planetID)` bind — the browser sends the target planetID, the BFF binds it and holds the OID handle. WB-BEYONCE → NEW `dispatchBoundBeyonceWrite(req,res,next,method,args,kwargs)` → `readHeldFlight` (recover the session's own live solarSystemID) + `requireInSpace` then `boundCall(held, webSessionID, parkBindSpec(before.flight.solarSystemID), method, args, kwargs)` off the R5a beyonce remote-park bind (`Moniker("beyonce", solarSystemID)`, `BEYONCE_BIND_GROUP=5`). Both hold the OID BFF-side; NOT the top-level `/call` seam. **EXTRA-CARE (destructive, extra-explicit confirm, never-fire — nothing fired, no live server):** `UserAbandonPlanet` ("ABANDONS the colony — all structures lost, cannot be recovered").

**CONFIRM-GATE (how refuse-without-confirm is UNIT-tested, NOT live):** each BFF `POST /api/bridge/planet/*` and `/api/bridge/flight/*` route calls `requireWriteConfirmation` → refuses `400 CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED` (NO bind, NO flight read, NO bound call) unless the browser sends `confirm: true`; UserAbandonPlanet carries the extra-explicit destroy message. Proven by NEW `test/bridgePlanetBeyonceWrites.test.js` booting `createApp` against a FAKE recording gateway: all 11 routes answer 400 + `gateway.calls.bind.length === 0` + `gateway.calls.boundCall.length === 0` on the refusal path (unit test of the route handler, NOT a live curl).

**ROUTES (`src/server.js`):** `/planet/{network/update,commodities/launch,commodities/transfer,abandon}`; `/flight/{goto-point,goto-bookmark,abandon-loot,fleet-tag-target,jump-through-fleet,bookmark-location,bookmark-scan-result}`. **DECODERS — TWO NEW files (LOCAL coercions only — stayed off `market*.ts`):** `web/src/bridge/boundPlanetWrites.ts`'s `decodePlanetWriteAck` (serialized colony / [simTime,runTime] pair / null → `{ok,applied,result}`) + `boundBeyonceWrites.ts`'s `decodeBeyonceWriteAck` (null / bookmark-id, plus the post-write `flight` snapshot the beyonce dispatch re-reads → `{ok,applied,result,flight}`); educated guess (fast mode; NOT ONE write fired live).

**⚠ ARG-INJECTION — NO new flag added; the premise did not hold.** The R103 goal expected the planetMgr writes to be the "write realization" of the R77 read leaks #18-#20 (a browser reconfiguring/abandoning a FOREIGN colony). Reading the handlers: all four force `ownerID = getSessionCharacterID(session)` (`UserAbandonPlanet` → `abandonColony(planetID, session.characterID)`); the planetID is caller-supplied via the bind (`_resolvePlanetID`, allowArgs:false → `session._planetMgrLastPlanetID`) but the colony operated on is always `(planetID, SESSION-char)`. So — UNLIKE the READ leak where `GetFullNetworkForOwner(planetID, ownerID)` took a caller-supplied ownerID and returned a FOREIGN owner's colony — these writes CANNOT touch another player's colony; a caller can only build/launch/transfer/abandon its OWN colony on the chosen planet. Owner-gated → not the write realization of #18-#20, no handoff-doc append. The 7 beyonce writes all resolve ship/scene/char from the session (`CmdJumpThroughFleet`'s (charID, shipID) name a fleet-mate's bridge but are validated against the session's own fleet membership) → no foreign-id write, no flag.

## R104 — Phase-4 BOUND WRITES: probe / scan control (WB-SCAN 9) — signal-tracker register / probe reposition / range-step / cone (D-)scan / probe scan request / lost-probe reconnect / probe DESTROY / probe recover / probe activity-toggle (9) (2026-07-23)

**Fifth batch under the OPERATOR-OWNS-EVEJS protocol — NO server restart, NO live smoke.** Forty-seventh batch, nineteenth WRITE batch, tenth Phase-4, off ONE established bind (no new MachoBindObject pair). Client + bridge; 9 write pairs on existing handlers; no `Handle_*` change; NO panel/tab/store slice; market files untouched. **All 9 LANDED, none skipped** — every `Handle_*` grep-confirmed present in `server/src/services/exploration/scanMgrService.js`: `Handle_SignalTrackerRegister`(:1540), `Handle_SetProbeDestination`(:1592), `Handle_SetProbeRangeStep`(:1619), `Handle_ConeScan`(:1671), `Handle_RequestScans`(:1767), `Handle_ReconnectToLostProbes`(:1883), `Handle_DestroyProbe`(:1908), `Handle_RecoverProbes`(:1923), `Handle_SetActivityState`(:1950).

**BIND MECHANISM — ONE NEW dispatch helper, ONE existing SESSION-SCOPED bind.** NEW `dispatchBoundScanWrite(req,res,next,method,args,kwargs)` → `boundCall(held, webSessionID, systemScanBindSpec(), method, args, kwargs)` off the R72 `scanMgr.GetSystemScanMgr` bind — mirrors `dispatchBoundInventoryWrite`/`dispatchBoundPlanetWrite`. ⚠ **KEY SECURITY PROPERTY:** unlike a MachoBindObject bind, `GetSystemScanMgr` takes NO caller args (`systemScanBindSpec()` = `{key:"scanMgr", service:"scanMgr", method:"GetSystemScanMgr", args:[], kwargs:null}`) — it always binds the SESSION's OWN current-system scan manager (server-derived via `_ensureSystemParity`), so the bind CANNOT be pointed at a foreign system. The BFF holds the OID handle; the browser never sees it. NOT the top-level `/call` seam. **EXTRA-CARE (destructive, extra-explicit confirm, never-fire — nothing fired, no live server):** `DestroyProbe` ("DESTROYS that launched scan probe — it is gone and cannot be recovered").

**CONFIRM-GATE (how refuse-without-confirm is UNIT-tested, NOT live):** each BFF `POST /api/bridge/scan/*` route calls `requireWriteConfirmation` → refuses `400 CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED` (NO bind, NO bound call) unless the browser sends `confirm: true`; DestroyProbe carries the extra-explicit destroy message. Proven by NEW `test/bridgeScanWrites.test.js` booting `createApp` against a FAKE recording gateway: all 9 routes answer 400 + `gateway.calls.bind.length === 0` + `gateway.calls.boundCall.length === 0` on the refusal path (unit test of the route handler, NOT a live curl).

**ROUTES (`src/server.js`):** `/scan/signal-tracker/register`, `/scan/probe/{set-destination,set-range-step,reconnect,destroy,recover,set-activity}`, `/scan/cone-scan`, `/scan/request-scans`. **DECODER — ONE NEW file (LOCAL coercions only — stayed off `market*.ts`):** `web/src/bridge/boundScanWrites.ts`'s `decodeScanWriteAck` (null / directional-scan list [ConeScan] / recovered-probeID list [RecoverProbes] → `{ok,applied,result}`, `result` carried through untouched); educated guess (fast mode; NOT ONE write fired live).

**⚠ ARG-INJECTION — NO new flag added; probes are inherently session-scoped.** The goal flagged the caller-supplied probeIDs on SetProbeDestination/SetProbeRangeStep/DestroyProbe/RecoverProbes/SetActivityState. Reading each handler: every one resolves `characterID = this._getCharacterID(session)` and operates on `probeRuntimeState` KEYED on that characterID — `getCharacterSystemProbes(characterID, systemID)` (SetProbeRangeStep does a `.find` by probeID within the caller's OWN set → a foreign probeID returns null), `removeCharacterProbes(characterID, [probeID])` (DestroyProbe/RecoverProbes → a foreign probeID is simply not in the caller's set), `synchronizeCharacterProbeGeometry(characterID, …)` (SetProbeDestination/RequestScans), `setCharacterProbeActivity(characterID, …)` (SetActivityState). The characterID NEVER comes from caller args; a caller-supplied probeID only SELECTS among the session char's OWN probes and a probe launched by another char simply MISSES. So a caller cannot mutate or destroy another player's probes — session-scoped by construction, NO handoff-doc append. (ConeScan/SignalTrackerRegister/ReconnectToLostProbes resolve ship/scene/system from the session with no mutating caller id.)

## ⚙ SERVER PROTOCOL CHANGE (operator 2026-07-23): the OPERATOR now owns EveJS start/restart

**From R100 onward, workers must NOT start or restart EveJS (:26002) or the web BFF (:26500).** The operator restarts EveJS when they want (they're testing other things and don't want workers interrupting). Workers commit the gateway pairs + BFF routes and verify via the **isolated eve.js test** (`webGatewayServiceCall` — checks the allowlist array directly, no running server) + the **web `node --test`** suite (decoder + route unit tests, incl. the route-refuses-without-confirm assertion, which is a unit test not a live hit). **No live smoke / no server restart.** The orchestrator restarts the web BFF on the operator's request only. - **⚠ THE 301 WRITE PAIRS — need your explicit OK before I permit them.** The full enumeration found **588 plumbable pairs** (`docs/plumbing-worklist.md`): 287 reads + 301 writes. I am sweeping the **reads** autonomously (read-only, low surface, and what UI needs to display). They're catalogued and ready; **say "wire the writes" (or name which subset) and I'll proceed, confirm-gated.** Until then, reads only.
- **Scale note:** even the 287 reads are a marathon (~20-30 worker cycles). Reads are unambiguously safe and wanted, so I'm proceeding, but flagging the magnitude — if you'd rather I stop after a subset (e.g. the highest-value read clusters) say so.

- **R53 — the "four faction themes + light/dark" premise does not match the code.** The R53 goal (and the roadmap's design-system notes) assume the app already supports Caldari/Amarr/Gallente/Minmatar themes plus light/dark. It does **not**: there is one dark `@theme` palette in `styles.css` (27 `--color-*` tokens) and no theme-switching mechanism anywhere (no `data-theme`, `color-scheme` toggle, `setProperty`, or faction CSS). R53 touched zero color tokens, so nothing was broken — but if faction/light-dark theming is expected to exist, it is **unbuilt work**, not a regression. Flagging so it can be scheduled if wanted.
- **R53 — a dead Tailwind `.rounded` utility ships in the bundle.** `public/dist` CSS contains `.rounded{border-radius:.25rem}`, generated purely because Tailwind's scanner treats the English word "rounded" in code comments as a class candidate. No element uses it, so no corner is actually rounded. If you want the bundle itself grep-clean of any non-zero radius, the fix is a Tailwind content/scanner config change (out of R53's CSS-only scope) — say the word and it can be done separately.
- **R53 — sub-40px "true EVE" density is available on request.** R53 kept every control ≥40px (R8). If you judge the result still not dense enough and want genuinely EVE-sized controls, that is an explicit R8-relaxation decision for you; it is a small, per-control change once you approve it.
